[2025-01-02T14:18:02] Ivan: ### 🚀 **Unified Power Apps Project: "Company Operations Hub"**  

**📚 Project Overview:**  
Build a **"Company Operations Hub"**, a centralized Power Apps solution integrating multiple sub-projects. Each sub-project will focus on a specific Power Apps functionality, making it an excellent learning journey while delivering a cohesive application.

---

## 🗂️ **Project Structure: Sub-Projects Breakdown**

### **1️⃣ Employee Directory Module** – *Focus: Data Display & Search*  
- **Purpose:** Enable users to search and view employee profiles.  
- **Power Apps Focus:** Display galleries, search functionality, and filters.  
- **Core Features:**  
   - Search employees by name or department  
   - Display profile details (photo, role, contact info)  
- **Data Source:** SharePoint List or Excel  

**✅ Key Learning:** Galleries, Filters, Search Box  

---

### **2️⃣ Leave Request Module** – *Focus: Forms & Approvals*  
- **Purpose:** Allow employees to request leaves with an approval workflow.  
- **Power Apps Focus:** Forms, patching data, workflow automation via Power Automate.  
- **Core Features:**  
   - Submit leave requests via a form  
   - Approval/Decline flow (Power Automate)  
   - Display approved leaves on a calendar  
- **Data Source:** SharePoint List or Dataverse  

**✅ Key Learning:** Forms, Power Automate Integration  

---

### **3️⃣ Expense Tracker Module** – *Focus: Attachments & Validation*  
- **Purpose:** Enable employees to submit and track expense claims.  
- **Power Apps Focus:** Attachments, form validation, conditional formatting.  
- **Core Features:**  
   - Submit expenses with receipt attachments  
   - Approval workflow for managers  
   - Visual expense summary  
- **Data Source:** SharePoint List or Excel  

**✅ Key Learning:** Attachments, Validation Rules  

---

### **4️⃣ Inventory Management Module** – *Focus: Barcode Scanning & Alerts*  
- **Purpose:** Manage product inventory efficiently.  
- **Power Apps Focus:** Barcode scanning, notifications, dynamic views.  
- **Core Features:**  
   - Scan product barcodes to update stock  
   - Set alerts for low stock levels  
   - Real-time inventory dashboard  
- **Data Source:** Dataverse or SQL Database  

**✅ Key Learning:** Barcode Scanner, Dynamic Notifications  

---

### **5️⃣ Helpdesk Ticketing Module** – *Focus: Status Updates & Notifications*  
- **Purpose:** Create and manage support tickets for IT or HR.  
- **Power Apps Focus:** Dynamic forms, status tracking, notifications.  
- **Core Features:**  
   - Create support tickets with priority levels  
   - Track status (Open, In Progress, Closed)  
   - Automated email notifications  
- **Data Source:** Dataverse or SharePoint  

**✅ Key Learning:** Status Tracking, Notifications  

---

### **6️⃣ Feedback Collection Module** – *Focus: Forms & Data Visualization*  
- **Purpose:** Gather and visualize customer or employee feedback.  
- **Power Apps Focus:** Forms, data visualization (integrated with Power BI).  
- **Core Features:**  
   - Collect feedback via forms  
   - Display analytics dashboard in Power BI  
- **Data Source:** SharePoint List or Dataverse  

**✅ Key Learning:** Forms, Integration with Power BI  

---

## 📊 **7️⃣ Admin Dashboard Module** – *Focus: Aggregation & Insights*  
- **Purpose:** Provide a centralized view of all sub-project data.  
- **Power Apps Focus:** Data aggregation, role-based access control (RBAC).  
- **Core Features:**  
   - Consolidated dashboard for HR, Admin, and Managers  
   - Overview of leave requests, tickets, expenses, and inventory  
- **Data Source:** Combined datasets from previous modules  

**✅ Key Learning:** Role-Based Access, Aggregated Views  

---

## 🔄 **Workflow Automation via Power Automate**  
- Automate notifications for leave approvals and expense submissions.  
- Create reminders for low inventory stock.  
- Schedule monthly reports for feedback summaries.  

---

## 🛠️ **Integration Points**  
- **Data Sources:** SharePoint, Excel, Dataverse, SQL  
- **Notifications:** Power Automate Emails  
- **Analytics:** Power BI for advanced reporting  

---

## 🎯 **Learning Outcomes:**  
- Explore core Power Apps components: Forms, Galleries, Scanners, and Flows.  
- Learn to integrate with Power Automate and Power BI.  
- Understand data storage and manipulation using SharePoint and Dataverse.  
- Build a modular and scalable app structure.  

---

## 🚦 **Suggested Development Roadmap:**  
1. Start with **Employee Directory** for a foundational understanding.  
2. Progress to **Leave Requests** and **Expense Tracker** for forms and approval workflows.  
3. Tackle **Inventory Management** and **Helpdesk Ticketing** for advanced components.  
4. Conclude with the **Admin Dashboard** for data aggregation and role management.  

---

This **"Company Operations Hub"** combines practicality with learning while offering real-world utility. Are there specific functionalities you’d like to prioritize? 😊

[2025-12-20T23:44:44] Ivan: UHere are the calculated quantities to feed 9 Humans + make enough extra sauce to marinate a second batch of eggs later.
Since you are serving a large group, I have scaled the measurements to be "Kitchen Safe" (slightly generous so you don't run out).
🛒 The Shopping List (Quantities for 9 People)
 * Milanesas: 9 large ones (or 14-15 small ones).
 * Bacon: 500g - 700g (Approx 2 packs).
 * Noodles: 3 to 4 packets (approx. 1 kg dry weight).
 * Miso Paste: 1 whole tub (you will use about 400g).
 * Bean Sprouts: 1 kg (Large bag).
 * Corn: 3 cans.
 * Butter: 100g (cut into 9 cubes).
 * Green Onions: 3 large bunches.
 * Broth: 4 Liters (approx 450ml per person).
Phase 1: The "Mega-Batch" Master Liquid
This makes enough for the Soup Base AND to marinate your 11 eggs + plenty extra for new eggs.
Mix in a large Pitcher or Bowl:
 * 3 cups Soy Sauce
 * 1 ½ cups Soju
 * ¾ cup Rice Vinegar
 * ¾ cup Sugar
 * 5 tbsp Chopped Garlic
 * 2 tbsp Grated Ginger (Optional)
Action:
 * Whisk until sugar dissolves.
 * Divide:
   * Pour 4 cups into a container for your Eggs (The 11 current ones + future ones). Submerge the eggs now.
   * Keep the rest (~2 cups) for the Miso Tare (Step 2).
Phase 2: The Miso Tare (Soup Base for 9)
 * In a medium bowl: Empty your Miso Paste (approx 1.5 cups or 350g).
 * Add: The remaining ~2 cups of Master Liquid from Phase 1.
 * Mix: Whisk until it is a smooth, pourable dark paste.
Phase 3: The Bacon & Aroma Oil (Batch Cook)
You need a lot of oil for 9 bowls.
 * Render: Chop all 500g+ Bacon into squares. Fry in a large pan/wok.
 * Crisp: When bacon is crispy, remove bacon bits to a plate. Keep fat in pan.
 * Oil: Add 2 chopped Onions + 1 whole head of chopped Garlic to the hot bacon fat. Fry until golden.
 * Finish: Turn off heat. Add ½ cup Sesame Oil. Pour into a serving jar/mug.
 * Honey Bacon: Put bacon bits back in the warm pan. Drizzle 3-4 tbsp Honey. Toss.
Phase 4: The Assembly Line (How to Serve 9 People)
Cooking for 9 is hard because noodles get cold. Do this in "Waves" or get a helper.
Station Setup:
 * Bowl Station: 9 Bowls lined up.
 * Topping Station: Corn, Butter, Bacon, Green Onions, Eggs (Halved), Milanesas (Sliced).
The Workflow:
 * Prep the Bowls: Put 2 heaping tablespoons of the Miso Tare (Phase 2) into every bowl.
 * The Broth: Ensure your 4 Liters of Broth are boiling furiously.
 * The Noodles (Batch Cook):
   * Unless you have a giant pot, cook noodles in 3 batches of 3 portions.
   * Don't overcrowd the pot or noodles gets soggy.
 * Serve:
   * Pour hot broth into 3 bowls. Whisk.
   * Add noodles to those 3 bowls.
   * Pass to helper to add toppings (Sprouts -> Milanesa -> Egg -> Corn -> Butter -> Bacon -> Green Onion -> Oil).
   * Repeat for the next batch.
Enjoy your feast!

Acá va el breakdown completo para servicio a domicilio:

LISTA DE PRECIOS - DEPILACIÓN IPL ICE A DOMICILIO

SESIÓN INDIVIDUAL:

Zonas Minis (bozo, mentón, patillas, dedos):
- En local: Gs 25.000
- A domicilio: Gs 40.000

Axilas:
- En local: Gs 40.000
- A domicilio: Gs 55.000

Bikini simple:
- En local: Gs 45.000
- A domicilio: Gs 60.000

Bikini completo / cavado:
- En local: Gs 55.000
- A domicilio: Gs 75.000

Brazos completos:
- En local: Gs 60.000
- A domicilio: Gs 80.000

Media pierna:
- En local: Gs 65.000
- A domicilio: Gs 85.000

Pierna entera:
- En local: Gs 100.000
- A domicilio: Gs 130.000

Espalda / abdomen:
- En local: Gs 80.000
- A domicilio: Gs 105.000

Cuerpo completo:
- En local: Gs 170.000
- A domicilio: Gs 220.000

PACKS A DOMICILIO (6 sesiones, 20% off):

- Axilas x6: Gs 265.000 (ahorra Gs 65.000)
- Bikini completo x6: Gs 360.000 (ahorra Gs 90.000)
- Pierna entera x6: Gs 625.000 (ahorra Gs 155.000)
- Cuerpo completo x6: Gs 1.055.000 (ahorra Gs 265.000)

COMBOS POPULARES A DOMICILIO (sesión):

Combo 1 - Básico (axilas + bikini simple):
Gs 100.000 (ahorrás Gs 15.000)

Combo 2 - Media (axilas + bikini + media pierna):
Gs 175.000 (ahorrás Gs 25.000)

Combo 3 - Premium (axilas + bikini completo + pierna entera + bozo):
Gs 280.000 (ahorrás Gs 40.000)

Combo 4 - Full Body:
Gs 220.000

RECARGO DOMICILIO EXPLICADO:
El recargo de Gs 15.000-50.000 por zona cubre:
- Transporte/combustible
- Tiempo de traslado
- Conveniencia para el cliente
- Es aprox 35-50% más que precio base

COSTOS OPERATIVOS POR SESIÓN:
- Electricidad del equipo: casi nulo
- Gel conductor (si usás): Gs 2.000-3.000
- Transporte: Gs 10.000-15.000 promedio
- Desgaste equipo: mínimo (999,999 flashes)
- Tu tiempo (1-2 hrs con traslado): tu costo principal

GANANCIA ESTIMADA:
Si hacés 3 clientes/día a domicilio con combo promedio de Gs 175.000:
- Ingreso diario: Gs 525.000
- Costos (~Gs 50.000 transporte + insumos): Gs 50.000
- Ganancia neta/día: ~Gs 475.000
- Mensual (22 días): ~Gs 10.450.000 (~USD 1,350)

TIP: Ofrecé descuento si 2+ personas del mismo domicilio se atienden juntas. Ahorrás viaje y ganás más por visita.
